    <div class="max-bound rhs-page-body">
    <div class="outer-pad">
      <div  data-history-node-id="38621" about="/programs/undergraduate/academics/academic-majors">
        
        <div class="editor-content">
          <h1>Academic Majors</h1>
                                <p>Undergraduates at Smith learn from world-class faculty who deliver a state-of-the-art curriculum based on the needs of the real world. With our&nbsp;<a href="https://www.rhsmith.umd.edu/programs/plus-1">Plus 1 program</a>, students have the option to earn their bachelor's and master's degrees in just five years for select majors.</p>
                  </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-umd_smith_page page-section-us_wrapper">
    <div class="max-bound">
      <div class="outer-pad">
          <div class="rhs-wrapper bg-umd-lighter-gray">
              <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_text">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11036">
				<h2><a id="accounting"></a>Accounting</h2><p>When you study Accounting at Smith, you’ll learn from faculty who influence&nbsp;<a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cd8dT0FuqQ4&amp;feature=emb_title">how companies choose to invest in cybersecurity</a>, and network with Smith accounting alumni, who hold more CEO and managing partner positions in public accounting firms than any other university.</p><p>The accounting curriculum includes financial planning, budgeting, accounting systems, financial management controls, financial analysis of performance, financial reporting, internal and external auditing, and taxation. It provides an excellent foundation for careers like public accounting, management, forensic accounting, and consulting.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_accordion">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      

	<div class="accordion" id="accordion-11046">
																																																																																																									<div class="card">
										<div class="card-header" id="accordion-11046-header-16">
												<button class="btn" data-toggle="collapse" data-target="#accordion-11046-content-16" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="accordion-11046-content-16">
							Degree Requirements / 4-Year Plan
							<br>
							<span class="accordion-item-summary"></span>
						</button>
					</div>
					<div id="accordion-11046-content-16" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="accordion-11046-header-16" >
						<div class="card-body">
							                
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11206">
				<h3>Download complete degree requirements and/or a 4-year plan.</h3><p><strong>New Requirements</strong></p><p><strong>Public Accounting (CPA) Track:</strong> Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. The Public Accounting Track is for students who are pursuing careers that require (or recommend) that they sit for the CPA (Certified Public Accounting) exam. Also, Accounting majors who want to keep the CPA career track open as an option should target the Public Accounting track. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="e32f8b2cd0bbe629520482519c3b41415"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1jfNXQkyh1MekPbTe0ObZ_ia-_QgTCu3a/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a></li><li data-list-item-id="ec9fba62ff2dc343c9f74ad84ed193e42"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1v8e3iCQbtn6j90NkhcQaLFNfkuG7uIaw/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a></li></ul><p><strong>Business and Accounting Advisory Track:</strong> Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. The Business and Accounting Advisory Track is for students who are pursuing careers in business and accounting that do not require the student to be CPA Exam eligible. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ef6a96ae561793d4e38d215c867305f6b"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1lul_RQfaaeX0G0FE2ROooAVc84KqcbN_/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a></li><li data-list-item-id="e36ed6d8b739e85211cf8599c078a582b"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1sxnwZal5e80gvW6AjavevxGAFvDS1Y_N/view?usp=drive_link" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a></li></ul><p><strong>Old Requirements</strong></p><p>- (New General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Spring 2014 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Spring 2014 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Please consult your Smith School Academic Advisor if you have questions.</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ea049980966872795f99e3acc1ee4c54c"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1kOUMaj51Nah2iW_OG3DZYjQju7GWiFhD/view?usp=share_link" target="_blank">Degree Requirements</a> [ PDF]</li><li data-list-item-id="e80df2ae1926269337f34f9c72d8f7978"><a href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/1shdiNV56ddEspwQZPN9wZdEKP3sh2uEx/view?usp=sharing" target="_blank">4-year Degree Plan</a> [ PDF ]</li></ul><p>- (New General Education) Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2012 through Fall 2013. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2012 have the option of adopting these requirements. External transfer students will be required to complete the new Smith School core requirements (launched in 2011) beginning Fall 2013. credits</strong></p></td></tr></tbody></table><h3>Suggested 150 Credit Hour Options</h3><p>Most states, including Maryland, require a minimum of 150 credit hours of college education to fulfill the education requirements to obtain a CPA license. Students can typically complete their Accounting degree and the specific course requirements for the CPA in the minimum 120 credits required to earn the degree. However, if 150 credits are required to fulfill the education requirements to obtain a CPA license, students must complete 30 credits beyond the specific course requirements to be licensed. These additional credits can be completed in several ways, as illustrated below. Please consult the Board of Accountants in the state where you intend to take the exam for their specific requirements because requirements can vary between states. For details on various states' CPA requirements visit <a href="http://www.nasba.org/" target="_blank">www.nasba.org</a>.</p><p><strong>Graduate Programs</strong></p><p>The Smith School offers several specialty master's degrees including one in accounting. Credits from these programs can apply to the 150 credits required for the CPA, and these programs can further enhance your accounting knowledge and skills or provide you with more depth in another area of business. In addition, accounting majors can pursue +1 Dual Bachelor's/Master's degrees and take up to 10 credits of graduate credit as an undergraduate at undergraduate tuition rates. In addition, some graduate credits can satisfy undergraduate degree requirements. For more details on these programs visit <a href="/programs/business-masters">Business Master’s</a> and <a href="/programs/plus-1">Plus 1</a>.</p><p>In addition, many institutions offer graduate programs for undergraduate accounting students. Consult institutions in your area about the availability of these types of graduate degrees.</p><p><strong>Double Majors/Degrees</strong></p><p>Accounting majors can select a second major in or outside the Smith School. Any credits required to complete the second major, above the 120 minimum required for a degree, can be applied toward the 150 total credits to obtain a CPA license. Students who accumulate 150 credits while completing both majors are eligible to earn two degrees upon their graduation.</p><p>Obtain information on <a href="https://academiccatalog.umd.edu/undergraduate/programs/" target="_blank">majors outside the Smith School</a>.</p><p><strong>Minors</strong></p><p>The Smith School of Business currently <a href="/programs/undergraduate/academics/academic-minors">offers two minors</a> that are open to Smith School students, Business Analytics and Innovation &amp; Entrepreneurship. Additionally, <a href="https://www.admissions.umd.edu/explore/colleges-and-schools/majors/minors" target="_blank">many minors are offered in other departments across the campus</a>.</p><p><strong>Smith Undergraduate Fellows Program</strong></p><p>The <a href="https://www.rhsmith.umd.edu/programs/undergraduate/academics/fellows-programs">Smith Undergraduate Fellows program</a> is an innovative concept in undergraduate business education, offering a series of special academic programs—or tracks—that will create small communities of scholars within the larger Smith School community. Each specialized fellows program combines opportunities for action learning and professional development with rigorous in-depth academic coursework focused on cutting-edge issues affecting 21st-century business.</p><p><strong>Study Abroad Programs (summer, winter, and/or semester programs)</strong></p><p>Studying abroad is a great way to expand your understanding of other business and cultural environments, and to demonstrate an ability to work effectively in foreign environments. In addition to the campus <a href="https://marylandglobal.umd.edu/global-learning-all/study-abroad" target="_blank">Study Abroad Office</a>, the <a href="/centers-initiatives/global-business">Center for Global Business</a> offers exchange programs for Smith School students. Visit the links below for more details on all your program options and contact information.</p>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11056">
				<h2><a id="finance"></a>Finance</h2><p>Smith’s Finance majors learn from faculty with real-world experience, like <a href="/smith-profiles/journey-begun-africa-never-along-single-path">Professor Lemma Senbet</a>, whose international finance career has influenced policy in Africa and the careers of the students he’s mentored, who include a bank president and a chief economist at the SEC.&nbsp;</p><p>The finance curriculum will familiarize you with the institutions, theory and practice involved in the allocation of financial resources within the private sector. It provides an excellent foundation for careers involving financial analysis and management, investment analysis and portfolio management, investment banking, insurance and risk management, banking, and international finance.</p><p>While only MATH120 Elementary Calculus I or MATH140 Calculus I (MATH130 also accepted) is required for the finance major, students with a strong math background are encouraged to complete both MATH140 Calculus I and MATH141 Calculus II. In addition, these students are encouraged to complete MATH240 Introduction to Linear Algebra because the material in this course provides a deeper understanding of finance. Students with an advanced math background will be in a better position to compete for admission to the Quantitative Finance Fellows and the Wall Street Fellows programs. In addition, this quantitative background will expand a student's career opportunities within finance.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11076">
				<h2><a id="information-systems"></a>Information Systems</h2><p>The Smith School’s Information Systems (IS) major is ranked No. 6 in the country (and the school is No. 11 among publics) by U.S. News &amp; World Report and includes stellar faculty with real-world experience and influential research, like Louiqa Raschid, who is <a href="/news/raschid-receives-nsf-funding-improve-supply-chain-knowledge-networks-covid-19-medical-supplies">optimizing supply chain networks</a> for desperately needed medical equipment during the pandemic.</p><p>The IS major prepares you to effectively plan, use and manage information technologies and systems through a focus on system design and implementation skills, including database and web design. These core concepts provide an excellent foundation for careers in consulting, data analytics and information systems management.</p><p>View&nbsp;<a href="https://go.umd.edu/ishub-questions">common IS major questions</a>&nbsp;in the&nbsp;<a href="https://go.umd.edu/ishub">Smith Information Systems Hub</a>.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

    </div>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11096">
				<h2><a id="international-business"></a>International Business</h2><p>The International Business major dynamically pairs your interests in international economics systems and their inherent multicultural characteristics. In addition to the usual core course and electives that comprise the degree, you’ll have the opportunity to select an upper-level foreign language course to truly expand your skills in a global capacity.</p>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11116">
				<h2><a id="management"></a>Management</h2><p>When you select a Management major, you join a leading undergraduate program in management and entrepreneurship,&nbsp;<a href="/smith-profiles/edu-preneur-sparks-innovation-smith">led by “edu-preneurs” like Oliver Schlake</a>. Both of these programs are regularly highly ranked, and focus on leadership and entrepreneurship, engaging students in critical thinking and problem solving, which are applicable to many careers.</p><p>With a management major you have a wealth of potential career paths. These can include consulting analyst, financial advisor, human resource management, nonprofit management, and entrepreneurship, including social entrepreneurship.</p>
			</div>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11136">
				<h2><a id="marketing"></a>Marketing</h2><p>As a Smith marketing major, you’ll learn how to be successful at all levels of an organization by ensuring that quality goods and services are provided at fair prices and in a way that benefits the community and society. And you’ll learn from faculty like Rebecca Ratner, whose <a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uNAnHvHCUzs&amp;feature=youtu.be">research about solitude</a> has made a huge impact in consumer studies.</p><p>The marketing major’s curriculum lays an excellent groundwork for careers in brand management, advertising, consumer research, and sales.</p>

					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11156">
				<h2><a id="operations-management"></a>Operations Management &amp; Business Analytics</h2><p>The Operations Management &amp; Business Analytics major will equip you with the knowledge and skills to apply quantitative and statistically based modeling techniques to data. And with world-class faculty at the helm of your education, you’ll be able to drive and influence decision-making at all levels of business. These skills are in higher demand than ever before, and provide career opportunities in the public and private sectors, including careers in energy, finance, insurance, health care, logistics, and marketing.</p>

Essential Data and AI Skills for Business Analytics (CMSC/DATA320 Introduction to Data Science can substitute)<br>BMGT485 - Project Management (INST453 Project Management for Information Science can substitute)</p></td><td><p class="text-align-right">6 credits</p></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Total</strong></td><td><p class="text-align-right"><strong>18 credits</strong></p></td></tr></tbody></table>

    <div class="page-section page-section-umd_smith_page page-section-us_wrapper">
    <div class="max-bound">
      <div class="outer-pad">
          <div class="rhs-wrapper ">
              <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_text">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11176">
				<h2><a id="supply-chain"></a>Supply Chain Management</h2><p>Smith Supply Chain Management majors learn what it is to be the lynchpin holding an entire organization together. You’ll learn from renowned faculty with industry experience,&nbsp;<a href="/smith-profiles/leaving-industry-and-finding-something-more">like Adams Steven</a>, to ensure that the desired product is available to the customer in the right condition and quantity and at the right time, place, and cost. The curriculum incorporates state-of-the-art software applications, so you’ll learn to develop ways of increasing the efficiency of the flow of goods and services across organizations.</p><p>Supply Chain Management majors often double major with complementary majors like Operations Management, Marketing, Information Systems, International Business, and Finance.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="page-section page-section-us_wrapper page-section-ut_accordion">
    <div class="inner-pad">
      

	<div class="accordion" id="accordion-11186">

							<br>
							<span class="accordion-item-summary"></span>
						</button>
					</div>
					<div id="accordion-11186-content-16" class="collapse" aria-labelledby="accordion-11186-header-16" >
						<div class="card-body">
							                
	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--11426">
				<h3>Download complete degree requirements and/or a 4-year plan</h3><p><strong>New Requirements</strong></p><p>Effective for students entering UMD in Fall 2024 and later. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2024 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2020 have the option of adopting these new requirements. Students who entered UMD prior to Fall 2016 have the option of adopting these new requirements.   function addAriaToUMDLink() {
    const link = document.querySelector('.umdheader-main__logo a');

    if (link && !link.hasAttribute('aria-label')) {
      link.setAttribute('aria-label', 'University of Maryland homepage');
    }
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', addAriaToUMDLink);

  // Also watch for dynamically injected content
  const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
    addAriaToUMDLink();
  });

    childList: true,
    subtree: true
  });

    <main id="main-content">
        <div class="container">
            <div class="row">
                <div id="page" class="col-12">  
        <h1>Bachelor of Science in Information Science at College Park (InfoSci)</h1>
             <div class="breadcrumb">
                   <a href="/">Home</a>&nbsp;&nbsp; / &nbsp;&nbsp;
                   <a href="https://info.umd.edu/academics/bachelors-programs/">
                       Bachelors Programs                   </a>
                   </div>
               
<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-2-3"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><strong>The Bachelor of Science in Information Science (InfoSci) 4-year degree program at College Park</strong> teaches students skills in technical areas such as database design, information architecture, web/mobile development, data analytics, and cybersecurity alongside areas of the social sciences, leadership, and the humanities – addressing the growing and unique need for information professionals who understand complex social and organizational issues.</p>
<p>This innovative curriculum blends theory, practical skills, and hands-on learning with <a title="Industry Professionals at INFO" href="/directory/?job_type=adjunct-faculty&amp;expertise_areas=0">industry professionals</a>, preparing our graduates to apply their skills directly and immediately in their careers. Our students work on projects with the UMD INFO College’s 400+ top industry partners and have opportunities for hands-on learning at the college’s world-renowned <a title="UMD iSchool Research Centers and Labs" href="/research/centers-and-labs">research centers and labs</a>.</p>
<p>InfoSci graduates go on to careers in data science and analytics, database administration, User Experience/User Interaction, technology development, digital curation, cybersecurity, web content and development, systems architecture, and more.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-3 quicklinks"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h2 style="text-align: left"><span style="font-size: 13pt">Current InfoSci Students Quick Links</span></h2>
<p>INFO provides advising and support to current InfoSci students.<br />
<a class="btn btn-danger" title="New Students: Next Steps" role="button" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/new-students-next-steps-orientation/">New Students: Next Steps</a><br />
<a class="btn btn-danger" title="Advising &amp; Student Services" role="button" href="/academics/student-services/undergraduate-college-park/">Advising &amp; Student Services</a><br />
<a class="btn btn-danger" title="Curriculum" role="button" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/bachelor-of-science-in-information-science-college-park/curriculum-cognate-areas/">Curriculum</a><br />
<a class="btn btn-danger" title="Support Resources &amp; Financial Aid" role="button" href="/academics/scholarships-awards/">Support Resources &amp; Financial Aid</a><br />
<a class="btn btn-danger" title="Student Organizations" role="button" href="/academics/umd-info-college-student-organizations/">Student Organizations</a><br />
</div></div>
</div>
<div class="linewithtext">
<h2>Interested in a Bachelor of Science in Information Science at College Park?</h2>
</div>
<div class="su-row">
<div class="su-column su-column-size-2-3"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<iframe title="Bachelor of Science in Information Science (InfoSci) at University of Maryland, College Park" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/4NFe5SHektA" width="784" height="412" frameborder="0" allowfullscreen="allowfullscreen" aria-label="Video: Bachelor of Science in Information Science (InfoSci) at University of Maryland, College Park program overview"></iframe></p>
<p><a title="Licenciatura en Ciencias de la Información (InfoSci), Universidad de Maryland, College Park" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OSN3b1EmWoo" aria-label="Haga clic aquí para ver el video en Español.">Haga clic aquí para ver el video en Español. <i class="fa fa-external-link" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="sr-only">(external link, opens in a new tab)</span></a><br />
</div></div>
<div class="su-column su-column-size-1-3"><div class="su-column-inner su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<h4>Are you interested in joining the UMD InfoSci program? Learn how to Apply below!</h4>
<p><a class="btn btn-success" title="Request More Info" href="https://admissions.umd.edu/how-to-apply">Apply Now <i class="fa fa-external-link" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="sr-only">(external link, opens in a new tab)</span></a></p>
<p><a class="btn btn-success" title="Change Major" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/change-major/">Change Major</a><br />
</div></div>
</div>
<div class="su-accordion su-u-trim">
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>UMD Students Change of Major</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p>Current UMD students interested in declaring the BA in Technology and Information Design as a primary or secondary major* must complete the change of major process in TerpEngage. <a title="Change of Major Process" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/change-major/">Click here for step by step instructions</a>.</p>
<p>*Students wishing to earn a double major or a double degree may need additional advising and coordination with the advising office of their other major prior to course registration.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Curriculum</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p>The Bachelor of Science in Information Science (InfoSci) degree at College Park requires 120 credits, including 40 credits in General Education and 45 credits in the Information Science major. In addition to the ten core courses, students must complete 15 credits (five courses) of upper-level major electives to earn their degree in Information Science.</p>
<p>Benchmark Courses set students up for success in the program and create foundational knowledge. Courses include Precalculus, Elementary Statistics and Probability, Introduction to Information Science, and more.</p>
<p>Major Core Courses allow students to become experts in the field, with courses like Information Organization, Statistics for Information Science, Database Design and Modeling, and User-Centered Design.</p>
<p><strong>Visit the </strong><a title="InfoSci at College Park Curriculum" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/bachelor-of-science-in-information-science-college-park/curriculum-cognate-areas/">InfoSci at College Park curriculum</a><strong> page for more details.</strong></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Undergraduate Minors</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p>The INFO College also offers three academic minors to UMD undergraduate students. InfoDesign students can choose from these minors or others offered at UMD.</p>
<ul>
<li>The Technology Innovation Leadership Minor</li>
<li>The Information Risk Management, Ethics, and Privacy Minor, Science, Technology</li>
<li>Ethics and Policy (STEP) Minor</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Click the </strong><a title="INFO Undergraduate Minors" href="/academics/minors-and-scholars/">INFO Undergraduate Minors</a><strong> page for more details.</strong></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Reputation</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><strong>National Rankings</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>UMD is<strong> #1 in the USA</strong> in producing computer science and information science bachelor’s degrees among non-online universities (<a title="The Chronicle of Higher Education Information Science Program Ranking" href="https://president.umd.edu/news/best-u-s-news-ranking-in-umd-history" aria-label="The Chronicle of Higher Education Information Science Program Ranking, opens in a new window">The Chronicle of Higher Education</a>) [External Link] and <strong>#3 in the nation</strong> overall</li>
<li>UMD is <strong>#18 in the USA</strong> as a Public Research Institution (NSF HERD Survey)</li>
<li>UMD is <strong>#7 in the USA</strong> as a University for Undergraduate Entrepreneurship (The Princeton Review)</li>
<li>InfoSci is the <strong>fastest-growing</strong> undergraduate program at UMD</li>
</ul>
<p><a title="More Info for Prospective Students" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/prospective-students/">More Info for Prospective Students</a></p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Career Paths</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p>A bachelor’s degree in Information Science from the top-ranked UMD College of Information will equip you with the skills and credentials needed to launch a successful career as an information professional across all types of organizations – including government institutions, non-profits, educational and cultural institutions, the private sector, and the intelligence community.</p>
<p>INFO graduates have gone on to work at leading organizations like IBM, Google, the Library of Congress, National Archives, the Smithsonian, the CIA, IMF, Discovery Channel, the National Agricultural Library, the New York Times, and National Geographic, among others.</p>
<p>To help you prepare for and kickstart your career, the UMD College of Information offers a variety of resources for both students and alumni. Check out the <a title="INFO College Career Resources" href="/academics/career-resources/">Career Resources</a> and the <a title="INFO College Bachelor's Program Career Paths" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/career-paths/">Careers Paths</a> pages to learn more.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Student Community</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p>INFO’s faculty, staff, and 2800+ students come from over 61 countries and create a welcoming and encouraging environment. 56% of students identify as male, 45% female, 1% other; 63% of students identify Asian (29%), Black (24%), White (22%), Hispanic/Latino (9%), Biracial (4%), American Indian or Alaskan Native (&lt;1%), Pacific Islander (&lt;1%), Unknown/Other (12%). The INFO College is committed to bringing together many perspectives to foster deeper understanding and innovation.</p>
<p>At UMD students can also choose from hundreds of <a title="UMD Student Experience" href="https://studentaffairs.umd.edu/student-experience">student organizations</a> [External Link], including <a title="INFO Student Organizations" href="/academics/umd-info-college-student-organizations/">INFO student organizations</a>, service opportunities, and social activities.</p>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>FAQs &amp; Information Sessions</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><strong>FAQs:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><a title="Frequently Asked Questions for INFO Degree Programs" href="/academics/bachelors-programs/bachelor-of-science-in-information-science-college-park/frequently-asked-questions">Frequently Asked Questions (FAQs)</a></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Information Sessions:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><a title="Virtual Information Sessions" href="/academics/virtual-info-sessions/">Virtual Information Sessions</a></li>
</ul>
</div></div>
<div class="su-spoiler su-spoiler-style-default su-spoiler-icon-plus su-spoiler-closed" data-scroll-offset="0" data-anchor-in-url="no"><div class="su-spoiler-title" tabindex="0" role="button"><span class="su-spoiler-icon"></span>Tuition/Costs</div><div class="su-spoiler-content su-u-clearfix su-u-trim">
<p><strong>Tuition</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>In-State (Maryland resident) tuition is $5,904.50 per semester for full-time undergraduate students.</li>
<li>Out-of-State (Non-Resident) tuition is $20,593.00 per semester for Non-Resident full-time undergraduate students.</li>
<li>The Mandatory fees are a flat rate of $397.00 for 1-8 credit hours and $861.00 for 9+ credit hours.</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>View the detailed breakdown of undergraduate </strong><a title="UMD Tuition and Fees" href="https://billpay.umd.edu/UndergraduateTuition" aria-label="UMD Tuition and Fees, opens in a new window">tuition &amp; fees</a> [External Link].</p>
<p><strong>Financial Aid</strong></p>
<p>The UMD INFO College provides financial aid to select current students who demonstrate academic excellence. Students may also reduce their tuition costs by serving as a TA or GA at UMD, but please note that these positions are competitive. There are also additional sources of financial aid offered by the University and by outside sources.</p>
<p><strong>Learn more about </strong><a title="Financial Aid Resources" href="/academics/scholarships-awards/">financial aid here</a><strong> and check out the </strong><a title="Scholarship Management Tool" href="https://umd.scholarshipuniverse.com/student/dashboard" aria-label="UMD Scholarship Management Tools, opens in a new window">UMD Scholarship Management Tool</a> [External Link].</p>
</div></div>
</div>
<p class="text-align-center" style="text-align: center"><a class="btn btn-success" title="Apply Now" href="https://www.admissions.umd.edu/apply/">Apply Now <i class="fa fa-external-link" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="sr-only">(external link, opens in a new tab)</span></a> <a class="btn btn-success" title="Contact the UMD INFO Academics Team" href="/contact/">Contact Us</a></p>

<div id="block-umd-ds-sub-pagetitle" class="block block-core block-page-title-block">
  
    
      
<h1><span class="field field--name-title field--type-string field--label-hidden">Graduation Plans</span>
</h1>


  </div>
<div id="block-umd-ds-sub-content" class="block block-system block-system-main-block">
  
    
      <div>
	<div >
		<div class="editor-content node-page-body">
							<p>All CMNS undergraduates are expected to create a semester-by-semester course plan toward graduation. Information and resources for making graduation plans such as major requirements, curriculum sheets and sample graduation plan templates are available on the website for each CMNS <a href="/undergraduate/future-students/majors-minors">major</a>. The CMNS <a href="/undergraduate/current-students/advising-academic-planning/peer-mentors">Peer Mentors</a> can assist students with making an individualized graduation plan.</p><p>In addition to major requirements, students should incorporate the following into their plans:</p><ul><li data-list-item-id="ef86bcb1790512d28f86dc5175b41fbde">Courses for <a href="https://academiccatalog.umd.edu/" target="_blank">minors</a> or <a href="https://academiccatalog.umd.edu/">certificate programs</a></li><li data-list-item-id="edd8c9258385578a0aeb4e2dac84f0968"><a href="https://globalmaryland.umd.edu/offices/education-abroad">Study abroad</a> courses</li><li data-list-item-id="eca6f22532278aa20efa13bf10740addd"><a href="https://ugst.umd.edu/llsop/index.html" target="_blank">Living &amp; Learning program</a> courses (Honors, Scholars, FIRE, etc.)</li><li data-list-item-id="ef2f59644bb325f49d2b86265dcb68e7d">Pre-professional program courses (pre-med, pre-dental, pre-law, etc.)</li></ul><hr><h2>Sample Graduation Plans</h2><p>Visit the links below to view the department webpages containing sample graduation plans and/or degree requirements. Questions about requirements should be directed to the departments.</p><p><a href="https://www.astro.umd.edu/education/undergraduate/astronomy-major" target="_blank">Astronomy</a><br><a href="https://aosc.umd.edu/education/undergrad-major/current-students/4-year-plan" target="_blank">Atmospheric and Oceanic Science</a><br><a href="https://chem.umd.edu/undergraduate/current-majors/general-information" target="_blank">Biochemistry</a><br><a href="https://bsci.umd.edu/curriculum">Biological Sciences</a><br><a href="https://chem.umd.edu/undergraduate/current-majors/general-information" target="_blank">Chemistry</a><br><a href="https://undergrad.cs.umd.edu/degree-requirements-cs-major" target="_blank">Computer Science</a><br><a href="https://ensp.umd.edu/specializations/biodiversity-and-conservation-biology/" target="_blank">Environmental Sciences &amp; Policy - Biodiversity and Conservation</a><br><a href="https://ensp.umd.edu/specializations/environmental-geosciences-and-restoration/" target="_blank">Environmental Sciences &amp; Policy - &nbsp;Environmental Geoscience and Restoration</a><br><a href="https://www.geol.umd.edu/undergraduate/ugprogram.php" target="_blank">Geology</a><br><a href="https://imd.umd.edu/academics/current-students/advising/computing-academic-planning" target="_blank">Immersive Media Design - Computing Track</a><br><a href="https://www-math.umd.edu/planning-your-courses.html" target="_blank">Mathematics</a><br><a href="https://neur.umd.edu/landingtopic/major-requirements" target="_blank">Neuroscience</a><br><a href="https://prehealth.umd.edu/explore-careers/nursing">Nursing Pathway</a><br><a href="https://umdphysics.umd.edu/academics/undergraduate/ugrad-requirements.html" target="_blank">Physics</a></p>
					
		</div>
				
	</div>
</div>
  </div>

  </div>

<section class="section section--intro section--padded-small">
    <div class="wrapper--ks">
        <div class="silc-grid silc-grid--collapse paragraph paragraph--type--intro-text paragraph--view-mode--default">
          
              <div class="silc-grid__col silc-grid__col--8-medium">
                                      <p>The College of Arts and Humanities is dedicated to helping students navigate and enrich their academic experience, and to Be WORLDWISE.</p>
                                </div>

              <div class="silc-grid__col silc-grid__col--4-medium">
                              </div>

            <!-- Render without, so quickedit/etc works -->
            

                  </div>
    </div>
</section>



<section class="section--padded-small">
  <div class="wrapper--ks">
    <div class="paragraph paragraph--type--html paragraph--view-mode--default">
            <div class="silc-grid silc-grid--collapse silc-grid--no-gutters">
        <div class="silc-grid__col silc-grid__col--8-medium">

          <!-- Sidebar if fields are present -->
                    <div class="section--sidebar">
          
            <!-- Image/caption if present -->
            
            <!-- Sidebar if present -->
                          <div class="sidebar__block ">
                                      <h3 class="fancy-header">Menu</h3>
                                                                          <p><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising">Advising</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/academic-plans"><strong>Academic Plans</strong></a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/academic-probation-and-dismissal">Academic Probation &amp; Dismissal</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/declaring-major">Declaring a Major</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/exceptions-policy">Exceptions to Policy</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/global-engagement">Global Engagement</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/graduation-commencement">Graduation &amp; Commencement</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/online-forms">Online Forms</a><br><a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/orientation">Orientation</a></p><p><br>&nbsp;</p>
                                                  </div>
            
          <!-- End sidebar if fields are present -->
                    </div>
          
          
          <h3>Academic Plans/Degree Requirements</h3>
<p>All University of Maryland students are required to complete a minimum of 120 credits and earn a minimum 2.0 cumulative GPA. They are also responsible for completing the General Education Requirements.</p>
<p>Additionally, students are responsible for completing the College of Arts and Humanities’ Requirements.</p>


          

        </div>
      </div>
          </div>
  </div>
</section>

  <section class="section--padded-small">
    <div class="wrapper--ks">
      <div class="paragraph paragraph--type--accordion paragraph--view-mode--default">

                  <div class="silc-grid silc-grid--no-gutters silc-grid--collapse">
            <div class="silc-grid__col silc-grid__col--10-medium">
                                            
                              <h3 class="header__h3 header--margin-bottom">College Requirements</h3>
              
              <div
                 class="paragraph paragraph--type--accordion paragraph--view-mode--default silc-accordion"
                id="accordion-2111"
                                >

                
                
<section class="silc-accordion__section" id="silc-accordion-7976">
  <header class="silc-accordion__header">
      <h2 class="silc-accordion__label">ARHU158</h2>
  </header>
  <div class="silc-accordion__content">
      <p>All ARHU first year students (excluding students participating in one of the following programs: Honors Humanities, Design Cultures &amp; Creativity, University Honors, Global Challenges and Solutions, Gemstone, Interdisciplinary Business Honors, Integrated Life Sciences, Advanced Cybersecurity Experience for Students, College Park Scholars, Jimenez-Porter Writers' House) must take ARHU158, Explorations in Arts and Humanities, during their first semester on campus.</p>

      
  </div>
</section>

<section class="silc-accordion__section" id="silc-accordion-7981">
  <header class="silc-accordion__header">
      <h2 class="silc-accordion__label">Upper Level Credits</h2>
  </header>
  <div class="silc-accordion__content">
      <p>All ARHU students, with the exception of students earning a Bachelor of Music or Bachelor of Music Education degree, must earn a minimum of 45 hours of 3xx-4xx level credit. Students earning a Bachelor of Music or Bachelor of Music Education degree should consult a Music advisor.</p>
<p>Effective fall 2024, all ARHU students entering the college, with the exception of students earning a Bachelor of Music or Bachelor of Music Education degree, must earn a minimum of 39 hours of 3xx-4xx level credit.&nbsp; Students earning a Bachelor of Music or Bachelor of Music Education degree should consult a Music advisor.</p>

      
  </div>
</section>

<section class="silc-accordion__section" id="silc-accordion-7986">
  <header class="silc-accordion__header">
      <h2 class="silc-accordion__label">Global Engagement Requirement</h2>
  </header>
  <div class="silc-accordion__content">
      <p>The ARHU <a href="https://arhu.umd.edu/academics/advising/global-engagement">Global Engagement Requirement</a> responds to the University Strategic Plan's call for students to "be prepared to live, work, and thrive in the current and future world environment." We believe that the development of cultural competence will result in the knowledge, skills and disposition needed to understand, communicate with, and effectively interact with people across cultures and is an integral component in an arts and humanities education.&nbsp; All ARHU students with the exception of students earning a Bachelor of Music or Bachelor of Music Education degree must complete the&nbsp;ARHU Global Engagement Requirement.</p>

      
  </div>
</section>


              </div>

              <!-- Render without, so quickedit/etc works -->
              

            </div>
          </div>
        
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <h1 class="post-header__title"><span property="schema:name">Four Year Plans</span>
</h1>


    </div>
  </div>


        <nav aria-label="breadcrumb" class="breadcrumb">
    <ol class="breadcrumb__items">
                        <li class="breadcrumb__item"><a href="/">Home</a></li>

    </section>
    
  
  <a id="main-content" tabindex="-1"></a>  <noscript>For full functionality of this site it is necessary to enable JavaScript. Here are <a href="http://www.enable-javascript.com/" target="_blank">instructions for how to enable JavaScript in your web browser</a>.</noscript>

        <div class="main-content compartment main-content--full-w">
          <div data-drupal-messages-fallback class="hidden"></div>


          
                    <div id="block-agnr-content" class="block block-system block-system-main-block">
  
    
      <div class="content">
      


<article about="/undergraduate/current-students/academic-advising-support/four-year-plans" typeof="schema:WebPage" class="page node-id-1900 full clearfix">
      <div class="section__inner section--padded-top">
      <div class="expand">
        <div class="page-hero page-hero--with-text ">
            <div class="page-hero__image" style="background: url('/sites/agnr.umd.edu/files/styles/optimized/public/2019-01/Four%20Year%20Plan%20Picture.jpg?itok=eSJ7I6Sl') center/cover;"></div><div class="page-hero__inner">
                                  <div class="page-hero__wedge"></div>
                  <div class="page-hero__text">
                    <h2 class="page-hero__title">Four Year Plans</h2>
                  </div>
                            </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  
      <div class="section__inner section--padded">
        <p>All students in the College of Agriculture and Natural Resources must develop a graduation plan (also called a "4-year plan") to map out all courses needed to graduate within an intended major and any minors, second majors, or special programs the student wishes to complete.&nbsp; In the drop down menus below, there is a curriculum workbook for each major containing three sheets:</p>

<ol>
	<li>
	<p>A summary curriculum worksheet -an overview of both general education requirements and major-required courses</p>
	</li>
	<li>
	<p>A listing of the major-required courses by course number and name</p>
	</li>
	<li>
	<p>A sample graduation plan for that major (an example of a plan that would meet all general education and major requirements)</p>
	</li>
</ol>

<p>The information in the curriculum workbooks is provided as advising tool to help students in planning their education and monitoring their progress to a degree. Factors such as the semester in which a student enters a particular major, changes in course sequence or offering times, availability of courses, satisfactory progress, and the like might necessitate changes to a student's plan. <em>No guarantee is offered or implied by the creation and use of a four-year plan</em>.</p>

<p>Students, if you have questions about the developing your plan or using the materials provided here, please talk to your academic advisor or your department's undergraduate program coordinator.</p>

<p><a class="btn btn--small" href="https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/1JHtgN8gdrgmZhE9Sip-hT43G3OeZtHEUboI-HEAei_A/edit?usp=sharing" target="_blank">Sample Blank Four Year Plan</a>&nbsp;<a class="btn btn--small" href="https://www.gened.umd.edu/students" target="_blank">General Education Information</a></p>

    </div>

<div >
	
	<div class="editor-content">
					<p>All of our undergraduate programs are designed to be completed within four years. Many students are interested in public health in a broad sense so we encourage you to review all of our four-year plans and checklists to see which major would be the best fit for you. We have walk-in advising hours as well as appointments that can be scheduled for prospective students if you would like to discuss any of these majors further.&nbsp;</p>

<p>Below you will find sample templates of how to structure your progress through any of our majors.&nbsp;</p>

<p>**Note**&nbsp; For each four-year plan that you review, you will see that I-Series courses and Diversity courses are not listed separately.&nbsp; As indicated on these templates, students should plan to take Distributive Studies courses that will double count for Big Question (SCIS),&nbsp;<em>formerly&nbsp;I-Series,</em> and/or Diversity courses if they are not already included as major requirements.&nbsp; For example, if a student chooses for to fill a Humanities course (DSHU) for the major, he/she should find a course that also satisfies the Big Question (SCIS),&nbsp;<em>formerly I-Series</em> or Understanding Plural Societies (DVUP).&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>
			</div>
</div>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--2601">
				<p><a href="https://umd.box.com/s/ct6ry7x117n405xkrqdvdrgps5egbode" target="_blank">Family Health 4 Year Plan and Major Curricula</a></p>

<ul>
	<li>Benchmark 1 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of two semesters into the major.</li>
	<li>Benchmark 2 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of three semesters into the major.</li>
	<li>Benchmark 3 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of four semesters into the major.</li>
</ul>

<p><strong>FMSC: Before Fall 2022</strong></p>

<ul>
	<li>Benchmark 1: PSYC100, SOCY100 or SOCY105 &amp; COMM107, INAG110, COMM125, or COMM100</li>
	<li>Benchmark 2: One FMSC elective &amp; STAT100 or EDMS451</li>
	<li>Benchmark 3: FMSC330 &amp; FMSC290, ECON200 or ECON201</li>
</ul>

<p><strong>FMSC: Fall 2026 &amp; Later</strong></p>

<ul>
	<li>Benchmark 1: Complete <strong>PSYC100 or SOCY100 or SOCY105&nbsp; </strong>with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of two semesters into the major.</li>
	<li>Benchmark 2: Complete <strong>FMSC330 and</strong> the<strong> Statistics Requirement </strong>with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of <strong><em>four semesters</em></strong> into the major.&nbsp;<br>
	<br>
	<br>
	&nbsp;</li>
</ul>

<p>&nbsp;</p>

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--12530">
				<p><a href="https://umd.box.com/s/whfrla0l8lmpz9t69w8vsr9ip0bg4x3h">Global Health 4 Year Plan &amp; Major Curricula</a></p>

<p><a href="https://umd.box.com/s/u1vk4beb32j31y9d4udv1a81xdzv70q2">SPH Policy Agreement (GBHL)</a></p>

<p>Benchmark 1 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of two semesters into the major.<br>
Benchmark 2 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of three semesters into the major.</p>

<p>&nbsp;</p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><strong>GBHL: Fall 2024 &amp; Later</strong></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>Benchmark 1: Complete&nbsp;<strong>GBHL200 and&nbsp;</strong></span><strong>2 other&nbsp;<span><span><span><a href="https://go.umd.edu/gbhl-curriculum" target="_blank">GBHL Supporting or Core</a></span></span></span><span><span>&nbsp;courses or&nbsp;</span></span></strong><strong>sets</strong>&nbsp;with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of&nbsp;<em><strong>two semesters</strong></em>&nbsp;into the major</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>Benchmark 2:&nbsp;Complete&nbsp;<strong>GBHL210,&nbsp;</strong><span><strong>BSCI170 and BSCI180 (or BSCI171), intermediate or higher world language,</strong></span>&nbsp;<strong>AND&nbsp;</strong><span><strong>4 other GBHL Supporting or Core courses</strong>&nbsp;</span><span>with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of&nbsp;</span><em><strong>&nbsp;four semesters</strong></em><span>&nbsp;into the major</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

																																<umd-element-accordion-item id="accordion-item-2606" data-visual-open="false" resize="" data-theme="light">
							<p slot="headline">Benchmark Review Process</p>
							<div slot="text" class="editor-content">
								                

<!-- THEME DEBUG -->
<!-- THEME HOOK: 'paragraph' -->
<!-- FILE NAME SUGGESTIONS:
   ▪️ paragraph--ut-text--default.html.twig
   ✅ paragraph--ut-text.html.twig
   ▪️ paragraph--default.html.twig
   ▪️ paragraph.html.twig
-->
<!-- BEGIN OUTPUT from 'modules/contrib/umd_terp_base/modules/ut_text/templates/paragraph--ut-text.html.twig' -->

	<div>
					<div class="editor-content" id="umd_terp_paragraph--2605">
				<p>Major benchmarks are your responsibility to understand, remember, and schedule.&nbsp; Missing a benchmark may result in dismissal from your major so it is important to take them seriously.&nbsp; Please note, some benchmarks have changed depending on the semester you declared your major.&nbsp;</p>

<p>The School of Public Health has a committee that reviews benchmarks every semester.&nbsp; If you are not enrolled in or missing a benchmark course, you will be contacted by the CASA office.&nbsp; If you wish to remain in your current SPH major, you will have the opportunity to appeal this missing benchmark through the appeal process. The appeal form will allow you to explain what benchmark course(s) you are missing and provide justification as to why you should be able to continue in the major.&nbsp; Note that not knowing, forgetting or choosing to prioritize other courses are not valid reasons for missing your benchmarks.&nbsp; The committee is looking for extenuating circumstances as to why you did not meet the benchmark.&nbsp; If you are concerned about meeting your benchmarks, you should&nbsp;<a href="https://terpengage.force.com/community/s/advisingpool/a0M2M00000hJrVTUA0/sph-casa-team-advising">meet with an academic advisor</a>&nbsp;as soon as possible.&nbsp;&nbsp;</p>

<p>Benchmark timelines are as follows:</p>

<ul>
	<li>Benchmark 1 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of two semesters into the major.</li>
	<li>Benchmark 2 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of three semesters into the major.</li>
	<li>Benchmark 3 must be completed with grades of “C-” or higher by the end of four semesters into the major.</li>
</ul>

<p>For benchmark related questions or concerns email <a href="mailto:sphbenchmarks@umd.edu">sphbenchmarks@umd.edu</a>.</p>
			</div>
				
	</div>

<!-- END OUTPUT from 'modules/contrib/umd_terp_base/modules/ut_text/templates/paragraph--ut-text.html.twig' -->

<div class="section silc-mtop--5 silc-mbottom--6">
    <div class="compartment compartment--full">
        <h1>Plans of Study</h1>
        <div class="breadcrumb">
            
	
  <ol class="breadcrumb__list">
          <li class="breadcrumb__list-item">
                  <a href="/">Home</a>
              </li>
          <li class="breadcrumb__list-item">
                  <a href="/your-education">Your Education</a>
              </li>
          <li class="breadcrumb__list-item">
                  <a href="/your-education/undergraduate">Undergraduate</a>
              </li>
          <li class="breadcrumb__list-item">
                  Plans of Study
              </li>
      </ol>


        </div>
            </div>
</div>
<div class="section silc-mtop--0">
    <div class="compartment compartment--full">
      <div class="silc-display--flex-800">

        <!-- Sidebar Nav -->
                  
<nav class="sidebar-nav" aria-label="Sidebar Navigation">
            <ul>
            <li>
                                    <a class="sidebar-nav__link" href="/your-education/undergraduate">Undergraduate</a>
                                

              <ul>
                    <li>
        <a href="/your-education/undergraduate/bachelor-arts-public-policy" class="sidebar-nav__link" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/536">Bachelor of Arts in Public Policy</a>
              </li>
                <li>
        <a href="/your-education/undergraduate/bachelor-arts-global-and-foreign-policy" class="sidebar-nav__link" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/14438">Bachelor of Arts in Global and Foreign Policy</a>
              </li>
                <li>
        <a href="/your-education/undergraduate/minors" class="sidebar-nav__link" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/551">Minors</a>
              </li>
                <li>
        <a href="/your-education/undergraduate/co-curricular-programs" class="sidebar-nav__link" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/641">Co-Curricular Programs</a>
              </li>
                <li>
        <a href="/your-education/undergraduate/plans-study" class="sidebar-nav__link sidebar-nav__link--active is-active" data-drupal-link-system-path="node/10089" aria-current="page">Plans of Study</a>
              </li>
        </ul>
  


            </li>
        </ul>
    </nav>
        
        <div class="page-content">

                      <figure class="page-content__hero">
              
    <img loading="lazy" src="/sites/default/files/styles/spp_featured_image/public/2019-07/feature_taliaferrodetail_06282019.jpg?itok=Cmfz8hiP" width="1100" height="445" alt="Taliaferro Hall" />






                          </figure>
          
          
          
  
  <section>
      <p>Undergraduate students in the School of Public Policy are encouraged to develop an academic plan to map out all courses needed to graduate with a&nbsp;policy major. Our&nbsp;<a href="https://spp.umd.edu/info/students/undergraduate#declare">undergraduate academic advising team</a>&nbsp;is available to work with students throughout this process. Sample four-year and two-year plans are available below.&nbsp;These materials may be downloaded to assist both current and prospective students in planning their education and monitoring their progress to a degree.</p><p>This information is to be used&nbsp;as an advising tool. The plan will require periodic modification as a result of the semester in which a student enters a particular major, changes in course sequence or offering times, availability of courses, satisfactory progress, etc.&nbsp;<em>No guarantee is offered or implied by the creation and use of a four-year or two-year plan</em>. Development of your individual plan and all changes thereafter should be made in consultation with your assigned academic advisor.</p><p>If you have questions about the development of your plan of study&nbsp;or use of the materials provided here, consult with your academic advisor.</p>
  </section>

  
  
  <div class="silc-accordion" id="accordion-2b3119cd-a5da-4ecb-bd74-74beb27fa266"   data-silc-accordion-animated>

    </button>

    <div id="block-system-main-block" class="block block-system block-system-main-block">
  
    
      <article about="/student-resources/student-services/coe-undergraduate-studies-student-services-office/major-four" typeof="schema:WebPage" class="node node--type-page node--view-mode-full">

  
    <span property="schema:name" content="Major Four Year Plans" class="rdf-meta hidden"></span>


    <div class="node__content node__wrapper">
                <h2 class="node__title">Major Four Year Plans      </h2>
          
                <div class="paragraph__tabs paragraph paragraph-tabs">
        <nav>
    <div class="nav nav-tabs" id="tabNav3649" role="tablist">
                        <button class="nav-link active" id="tabNav2603" data-bs-toggle="tab" data-bs-target="#tabContent2603" type="button" role="tab" aria-controls="tabContent2603" aria-selected="true">
            4 Year Plans and Benchmarks 
          </button>
                        <button class="nav-link" id="tabNav2604" data-bs-toggle="tab" data-bs-target="#tabContent2604" type="button" role="tab" aria-controls="tabContent2604" aria-selected="false">
            General Information and Program Requirements
          </button>
          </div>
  </nav>
  <div class="tab-content" id="tabContent3649">
        <div class="tab-pane show active" id="tabContent2603" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tabNav2603">
    


            <div class="field field--name-field-tab-content field--type-text-long field--label-hidden wysiwyg field__item"><p>The College of Education confers the degrees of Bachelor of Arts (B.A.) or Bachelor of Science (B.S.) depending on the amount of liberal arts study included in a particular degree program. Minimum requirements for graduation are 120 semester hours. Some majors require more than 120 credits. In addition to the university's general education requirements and the specific requirements for each program, the College of Education requires that all teacher education majors complete a Foundations of Education course and, depending upon the teacher education major, six to twelve semester hours of reading course requirements. Unless otherwise stated a&nbsp;grade of 'C-'&nbsp;or better is required in all pre-professional and professional course work required for the major. An overall Grade Point Average (GPA) of 2.75 must be maintained after admission to the Professional Teacher Education programs. A grade of "S" is required in the student teaching internship.</p><table class="table tablesaw tablesaw-stack" data-tablesaw-mode="stack" data-tablesaw-minimap><thead><tr><th>4 Year Plans and Benchmarks</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/2024/04/ART%204%20Year%20Major%20Bench_2401_0.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-entity-type="file" data-entity-uuid="be960b40-9a1d-4924-a122-7a61c8f0d53b" data-entity-substitution="file" title="ART 4 Year Major Bench_2401.pdf">Art Education</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/inline-files/Early%20Childhood%20Early%20Childhood%20Special%20Education%204%20Year%20Major%20Bench%20_%202401_0.pdf" target="_blank">Early Childhood/Early Childhood Special Education</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/inline-files/Elementary%20Education.pdf" data-entity-type="file" data-entity-uuid="fe9cc441-d201-4adb-8e24-7632b4797e1e" title="Elementary Education.pdf">Elementary Education</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/inline-files/Human%20Development%204%20Year%20Major%20Bench%20_%202601.pdf" data-entity-type="file" data-entity-uuid="481b755f-7c66-4962-833e-d979f338b890" title="Human Development 4 Year Major Bench _ 2601.pdf">Human Development</a>&nbsp;</td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/2025/09/Middle%20School%204%20Year%20Major%20Bench_%202508_ud.pdf" data-entity-type="file" data-entity-uuid="c703d265-3d9a-4325-bb68-3c7698d0949f" data-entity-substitution="file" title="Middle School 4 Year Major Bench_ 2508_ud.pdf">Middle School Math and Science Education</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/2024/04/English%20Ed%204%20Year%20Major%20Bench_%202401_0.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-entity-type="file" data-entity-uuid="35fedb65-faad-494f-8e33-0fe0aba37c02" data-entity-substitution="file" title="English Ed 4 Year Major Bench_ 2401.pdf">Secondary English&nbsp;Education</a></td></tr><tr><td><a href="https://education.umd.edu/sites/default/files/uploads/2024/04/Math%20SecEd%204%20Year%20and%20Major%20Bench%20_%202401_1.pdf" target="_blank" Education</a>&nbsp;(P-12th Grade)</td></tr></tbody></table><h3>Pre-Professional Requirements:</h3><p>Students in the pre-professional Education majors must apply to their respective professional teacher education programs.&nbsp;The application process for the professional programs will vary depending on the Education major.&nbsp;Students must be fully admitted to their professional education program upon completion of 60 credits.&nbsp;Transfer students that matriculate to the University with advanced credit (e.g. 60 credits or more) will be given two semesters to meet these requirements.</p><p>For more information about our programs and requirements please&nbsp;<a href="mailto:ed-advising@umd.edu">contact our advisors</a>.</p></div>
